-- Classroom access is no longer a per-student permission managed from the
-- People page. Every student needs to reach /classroom to see the bootcamps
-- assigned to them; bootcamp visibility itself is controlled by classroom_items
-- assignments and enforced by BootcampGate, not by a global flag.
--
-- The column default is already true (see 20260416015637), so new students are
-- covered. This backfills any existing student whose classroom access was
-- revoked under the old broad-permission model.
UPDATE public.profiles
SET has_classroom_access = true
WHERE has_classroom_access = false;

export interface UserPermissions {

export type PermissionFlag = (typeof ACCESS_FLAGS)[number];

// Narrow session-death classifier. Only three conditions count:
//  1. supabase.auth.getSession() returns no session while context says user is set
//  2. Supabase query error with HTTP status 401
//  3. Error message mentioning JWT / token-expired / invalid_token explicitly
// Everything else (including RLS 403s and null profile rows) is legitimate
// permission-denied and must fall through to the normal LockedModule path.
function isSessionDeadError(err: any): boolean {
  if (!err) return false;
  if (err.status === 401) return true;
  const msg = String(err.message || "").toLowerCase();
  return (
    msg.includes("jwt") ||
    msg.includes("token is expired") ||
    msg.includes("token expired") ||
    msg.includes("invalid_token")
  );
}

type PermissionFlags = Omit<UserPermissions, "loading">;

const DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS: PermissionFlags = {
  has_bootcamp_access: false,
  has_classroom_access: false,
  has_analytics_access: false,
  has_schedule_access: false,
  has_practice_access: false,
  has_drill_access: false,
  has_waj_access: false,
  has_journal_access: false,
  has_flagged_access: false,
  has_chat_access: false,
  has_export_access: false,
  has_ta_access: false,
  has_activity_access: false,
  is_admin: false,
};

// Sentinel thrown by the query fn when the session is dead. The hook reacts to
// it once (sign out + /auth bounce) instead of navigating from inside the
// queryFn, which may run detached from a render.
class SessionExpiredError extends Error {}

export function useUserPermissions(): UserPermissions {
  const { user, authReady, signOut } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

    queryKey: ["user-permissions", user?.id],

    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,

    // (that path signs the user out).

export const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = 'contact@aspiringattorneys.com';

// Every door a student can be given, grouped the way the classroom rail groups
// them, and labelled in the words the rail uses. This list is the admin UI: a
// flag that is missing here has no toggle anywhere, which is how
// `has_journal_access` and `has_activity_access` came to be ungrantable while
// the rail happily padlocked Wrong answers and Activities against them.
//
// Keep it in step with `ALL_FLAGS` in supabase/functions/admin-manage-users.
// The server writes what it is told; if the client cannot name a flag, no
// amount of pressing "grant all" will turn it on where Joshua can see it.
export const FLAG_GROUPS = [
  {
    label: 'Practice',
    flags: [
      { key: 'has_drill_access', label: 'Drill' },
      { key: 'has_practice_access', label: 'Practice tests' },
      { key: 'has_activity_access', label: 'Activities' },
      { key: 'has_bootcamp_access', label: 'Bootcamps' },

    ],

] as const;

export const CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS = FLAG_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.flags.map((flag) => flag.key));
/**
 * What a paying student gets regardless of which template is applied. Templates

] as const;
/** Deduped: every configurable flag is now also a default-on candidate. */
export const ALL_FLAG_KEYS = [...new Set<string>([...CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS, ...DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS])];

export const ALL_FLAG_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {};
FLAG_GROUPS.forEach((group) => group.flags.forEach((flag) => {
  ALL_FLAG_LABELS[flag.key] = flag.label;
}));

export function accessTemplate(enabled: string[]): Record<string, boolean> {
  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = enabled.includes(key);
  for (const key of DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = true;
  return out;
}

import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";

const corsHeaders = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
};

// The primary admin account can never be demoted, so the system always retains
// at least one admin and can never be locked out of role management.
const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = "contact@aspiringattorneys.com";

const ALL_FLAGS = [
  "has_bootcamp_access",
  "has_classroom_access",
  "has_analytics_access",
  "has_schedule_access",
  "has_practice_access",
  "has_drill_access",
  "has_waj_access",
  "has_flagged_access",
  "has_chat_access",
  "has_export_access",
  "has_ta_access",
  "has_journal_access",
  "has_activity_access",
];

async function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {
  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;
  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;
  const anonKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!;

  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");
  const token = authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length);

  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey);
  const { data: claimsData, error } = await anonClient.auth.getClaims(token);
  if (error || !claimsData?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");
  const user = { id: claimsData.claims.sub as string };

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);
  const { data: roleData, error: roleLookupError } = await admin
    .from("user_roles")
    .select("role")
    .eq("user_id", user.id)
    .eq("role", "admin")
    .maybeSingle();

  if (roleLookupError) {
    console.error("verifyAdmin: role lookup failed", {
      user_id: user.id,
      error: roleLookupError,
    });
    throw new Error("Forbidden");
  }
  if (!roleData) throw new Error("Forbidden");

  return { admin, callerId: user.id };
}

function jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status,
    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });

      const url = new URL(req.url);
      const mode = url.searchParams.get("mode");

      // and for the case where a student needs to answer the intake again.
      //
      // Deliberately does not touch auth: the account, its password and its
      // work all survive. Nothing here deletes a student's practice history.
      if (action === "reset_onboarding") {
        const { class_id, lock = true } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {
          onboarding: null,
          onboarding_completed_at: null,
          onboarding_notified_at: null,
          display_name: null,
        };
        if (lock) {
          for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;
          // The three a locked account still needs: somewhere to stand, a way
          // to reach Joshua, and a way to book.
          updates.has_classroom_access = true;
          updates.has_chat_access = true;
          updates.has_schedule_access = true;
        }

        const { data: reset, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update(updates)
          .eq("class_id", class_id)
          .select("class_id, display_name, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at");

import { Lock } from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useUserPermissions } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";

/**
 * What a student sees when a route's feature flag is false.
 *
 * It renders as ordinary stage content, not a full-viewport takeover: both
 * shells mount this inside their <Outlet/>, so `min-h-screen` plus a hard zinc
 * background painted a slab over the frame it is meant to sit inside. Tokens
 * only, so it reads in light and dark — the old `text-zinc-300` landed on the
 * outline variant's light `bg-background` and came out at 1.44:1.
 *
 * The old copy ("Premium Content Locked ... contact administration") named no
 * one and led nowhere. A locked door has to say who opens it.
 */
export default function LockedModule() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center px-6 py-16">
      <div className="max-w-sm text-center">
        <div className="mx-auto mb-6 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
          <Lock className="h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>

        <h1 className="mb-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">
          Not open yet
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          This part of the classroom isn't in your plan right now. If that looks
          wrong, send Joshua a message and he'll sort it out.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">

            Back to your classroom
          </Button>

## assistant

Here is how it works today. Everything below is verified against the code on `origin/main` (which matches what I saw live at lsatprep.study/teacher/students in the in-app browser) unless marked otherwise.

## The data model

Access is 13 boolean columns on `profiles`, one per "door":

`has_drill_access`, `has_practice_access`, `has_activity_access`, `has_bootcamp_access`, `has_journal_access`, `has_waj_access`, `has_flagged_access`, `has_analytics_access`, `has_export_access`, `has_classroom_access`, `has_chat_access`, `has_schedule_access`, `has_ta_access`

- **Every column defaults to `true`.** A new signup gets everything the moment the profile row is created. There is no "pending approval" state.
- `has_classroom_access` is effectively dead as a control: a 2026-06-30 migration force-set it true for everyone, and the UI never turns it off (the student needs somewhere to land).
- Admins (`user_roles.role = 'admin'`) bypass every flag.

## How it's enforced

[useUserPermissions.ts](src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts) reads the 13 flags once per login (cached 5 minutes). [ProtectedRoute](src/components/ProtectedRoute.tsx) checks the route's flag; if false, the student sees [LockedModule](src/components/LockedModule.tsx) ("Not open yet... send Joshua a message"). The classroom rail also padlocks items against the same flags.

Consequence: **a revoke takes up to 5 minutes to land** for a student already signed in, because the flags are cached client-side. There's no server push.

## What you can actually do from the UI (live)

On `/teacher/students`, each student row has exactly one button:

- **Revoke All Access** (red): confirm dialog, then sets all 13 flags false except `has_classroom_access`. Student keeps login and home page; every door locks.
- **Grant Full Access** (green): sets all 13 true.
- The button state is derived: it shows "Revoke" if *any* flag other than classroom is on. So a partially locked student still reads as "has access".
- The row text shows only `Drill on/off`, so the summary tells you about one flag, not the account state.

Expanding a row gives Promote/Demote admin and **Reset onboarding** (wipes intake, name, and locks all doors except classroom/messages/schedule).

Both go through the `admin-manage-users` edge function (`apply_template` action), which writes the columns with the service role and returns the written row.

## What exists in code but is not reachable

- **Per-flag toggles and templates (Trial / Standard / Full)** live in [AdminDashboard.tsx](src/pages/AdminDashboard.tsx) and [adminUsers.ts](src/lib/adminUsers.ts), but `AdminDashboard` is not mounted on any route. The templates are defined and the server supports them; you just have no way to click them.
- The edge function also accepts a single-flag `toggle` action and a `bulk_user` action; the live UI only calls `apply_template`.
- My local checkout (`feat/calendar-strip-deeplink`) has an older version of this button labelled "Grant/Revoke Drill Access" that only flips `has_drill_access`. That is not what's deployed.

## Where it falls short as an on/off switch

1. **No neutral default.** New signups are fully on. If you want "off until I say so", that's a column-default change plus a signup-time decision, not a UI change.
2. **Binary and lossy.** Revoke flattens whatever custom mix a student had; Grant restores everything, not what they had before. No record of the prior state.
3. **No expiry or reason.** Nothing stores *why* or *until when*. A paused student and a former student look identical.
4. **No audit trail.** `profiles` just holds the current booleans; nothing logs who flipped what and when.
5. **Slow to take effect** (5-minute cache) and no way to force a sign-out.
6. **Row summary lies by omission** ("Drill on" while nine other doors are off).

If the goal is a clean "is this person a current student" switch, my recommendation is a single `access_status` (`active | paused | off`) with `paused_at`/`reason`, computed into the 13 flags server-side, plus an audit table and cache invalidation on change. The 13 columns stay for fine-grain, but the switch you reach for daily becomes one field with one meaning. Say the word and I'll write that up as a spec before touching anything.

import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { useClassroomLayout } from "@/components/classroom/shell/ClassroomLayout";
import { useClassroomHome } from "./useClassroomHome";
import ClassroomHomeView from "./ClassroomHomeView";
import "./home.css";

/**
 * Classroom Home. Reads the student's own record and renders the dashboard;
 * every route it opens already exists in the rail, so this page is a shortcut
 * into the classroom rather than a second navigation.
 */
export default function ClassroomHome() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { openSection } = useClassroomLayout();
  const { model, loading, markDone, clearMessage, completingKey } = useClassroomHome();

  return (
    <ClassroomHomeView
      model={model}
      loading={loading}
      onOpen={(route) => navigate(route)}
      onSeeAllWork={() => openSection("work")}
      onMarkDone={markDone}
      onClearMessage={async (conversationId) => {
        try {
          await clearMessage(conversationId);
        } catch {
          toast.error("Could not clear that message. Try again.");
        }
      }}
      completingKey={completingKey}
    />
  );
}

  const { user } = useAuth();

  const canAccess = React.useCallback(
    (...flags: PermissionFlag[]) =>
      permissions.is_admin || flags.length === 0 || flags.some((flag) => permissions[flag]),

import { useOrientation, OrientationCardView } from "@/components/classroom/OrientationCard";
import AnnouncementBanner from "@/components/classroom/AnnouncementBanner";
import ClassroomHome from "@/components/classroom/home/ClassroomHome";

/**
 * The classroom home: the student dashboard.
 *
 * One screen that answers, in order, what to do now, what is assigned, whether
 * the coach said anything, when the next session is, and what the week holds.
 *
 * Except before setup is finished. Then the intake owns the whole stage: a
 * student answering questions about their week should not be reading a week
 * they have not built yet, and the old split showed both at once. They begin
 * from the invitation, walk the questions one at a time, and the classroom
 * appears behind them when they are done.
 */
export default function Classroom() {
  const { loaded, completed, setCompleted, name, answers, save } = useOrientation();

  // Nothing until the profile read lands: drawing the home first and swapping
  // it for the intake a beat later is worse than a moment of quiet.
  if (!loaded) return null;

  if (!completed) {
    return (
      <OrientationCardView
        initialName={name}
        initialAnswers={answers}
        onSave={save}
        onDone={() => setCompleted(true)}
        startCollapsed
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <AnnouncementBanner />
      <ClassroomHome />
    </>
  );
}

{"rows":[{"def":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()\n RETURNS trigger\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SET search_path TO 'public'\nAS $function$\nBEGIN\n  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR\n     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR\n     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR\n     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR\n     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR\n     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR\n     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR\n     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR\n     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR\n     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR\n     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR\n     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR\n     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR\n     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'profiles: access flags, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled';\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$function$\n"}]}

  // One button, whole account: revoking shuts every door except the classroom
  // home page, so the student can still sign in and land somewhere truthful.

  const setAllAccess = async (person: PeopleRow, grant: boolean) => {
    const who = person.display_name || person.email;
    if (!grant && !window.confirm(
      `Revoke all access for ${who}?\n\n` +
      'They keep their login and their home page. Everything else — drill, ' +
      'practice, bootcamps, review, messages, schedule — is locked.',
    )) return;

    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};

    flags.has_classroom_access = true;

    const previous = users;
    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === person.class_id ? { ...user, ...flags } : user)));
    try {
      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { action: 'apply_template', class_id: person.class_id, flags },
      });
      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      toast.success(grant ? `${who} has full access` : `${who} is locked to their home page`);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      setUsers(previous);
      console.error('setAllAccess failed', { class_id: person.class_id, grant, error });
      toast.error('Failed to update access');
    }
  };

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import Classroom from "./Classroom";

const { orientation } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  orientation: {
    loaded: true,
    completed: false,
    setCompleted: vi.fn(),
    name: "Ada",
    answers: {},
    save: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  },
}));

vi.mock("@/components/classroom/OrientationCard", () => ({
  useOrientation: () => orientation,
  OrientationCardView: () => <div>Set up your classroom</div>,
}));
vi.mock("@/components/classroom/AnnouncementBanner", () => ({
  default: () => <div>Announcement</div>,
}));
vi.mock("@/components/classroom/home/ClassroomHome", () => ({
  default: () => <div>Your week</div>,
}));

describe("Classroom", () => {
  it("gives the whole stage to the intake until it is finished", () => {
    render(<Classroom />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Set up your classroom")).toBeTruthy();
    // The home used to sit underneath the intake, so a student answering
    // questions about their week was reading a week they had not built.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Your week")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Announcement")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("draws the classroom once setup is done", () => {
    orientation.completed = true;
    render(<Classroom />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Your week")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Set up your classroom")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("draws nothing until the profile read lands", () => {
    orientation.loaded = false;
    const { container } = render(<Classroom />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe("");
  });
});

description: Add realtime permission refresh and the migration for publication and trigger guard
command: python - <<'EOF'
p='src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts'
s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
s=s.replace('''import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";''','''import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";''')
s=s.replace('''  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const query = useQuery<PermissionFlags>({''','''  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const userId = user?.id;

  // The hook mounts once per ProtectedRoute, so the subscription is guarded by
  // a single shared channel per user: the first mount opens it, the last unmount
  // closes it. Without this, every gated page would hold its own socket.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authReady || !userId) return;
    const entry = acquireProfileWatch(userId, () => {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user-permissions", userId] });
    });
    return () => releaseProfileWatch(userId, entry);
  }, [authReady, userId, queryClient]);

  const query = useQuery<PermissionFlags>({''')
s=s.replace('''    queryKey: ["user-permissions", user?.id],
    enabled: authReady && !!user,
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,''','''    queryKey: ["user-permissions", userId],
    enabled: authReady && !!user,
    // Realtime (below) is the primary path for a revoke to land; the short
    // stale window and the focus refetch are the fallback when the socket is
    // down, so "Revoke All Access" is never more than a tab switch away from
    // taking effect. Cached data keeps `loading` false, so neither flashes.
    staleTime: 60_000,
    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,''')
s=s.replace('''class SessionExpiredError extends Error {}
''','''class SessionExpiredError extends Error {}

// One realtime channel per signed-in user, shared by every mounted consumer.
// An UPDATE to the student's own profiles row (an admin flipping a flag)
// invalidates the cached permissions, so a revoke takes effect on the next
// render rather than when the stale window runs out. RLS scopes the stream to
// the student's own row; profiles is in the supabase_realtime publication
// (migration 20260922120000).
type ProfileWatch = { channel: ReturnType<typeof supabase.channel>; listeners: Set<() => void> };
const profileWatches = new Map<string, ProfileWatch>();

function acquireProfileWatch(userId: string, onChange: () => void): () => void {
  let watch = profileWatches.get(userId);
  if (!watch) {
    const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
    const channel = supabase
      .channel(`profile-access-${userId}`)
      .on(
        "postgres_changes",
        { event: "UPDATE", schema: "public", table: "profiles", filter: `class_id=eq.${userId}` },
        () => listeners.forEach((fn) => fn()),
      )
      .subscribe();
    watch = { channel, listeners };
    profileWatches.set(userId, watch);
  }
  watch.listeners.add(onChange);
  return onChange;
}

function releaseProfileWatch(userId: string, listener: () => void) {
  const watch = profileWatches.get(userId);
  if (!watch) return;
  watch.listeners.delete(listener);
  if (watch.listeners.size === 0) {
    profileWatches.delete(userId);
    void supabase.removeChannel(watch.channel);
  }
}
''')
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)
EOF
cat > supabase/migrations/20260922120000_profiles_realtime_and_activity_guard.sql <<'EOF'
-- "Revoke All Access" has to land while the student is still signed in.
-- useUserPermissions caches the flags client-side; it now subscribes to the
-- student's own profiles row and refetches on UPDATE, which needs the table in
-- the realtime publication. RLS still applies to the stream, so a student only
-- ever sees changes to their own row.
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM pg_publication_tables
    WHERE pubname = 'supabase_realtime' AND schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'profiles'
  ) THEN
    ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.profiles;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- has_activity_access was added after guard_profile_admin_fields() was written,
-- so it was the one flag a student could flip on their own row through the
-- "Students can update own profile" policy. Close it.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR
     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR
     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR
     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR
     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR
     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR
     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR
     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR
     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR
     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR
     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR
     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR
     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR
     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR
     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'profiles: access flags, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled';
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$;
EOF
echo ok
[/external_agent_tool_call]

 */
export default function LockedModule() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();
''',''' * The old copy ("Premium Content Locked ... contact administration") named no
 * one and led nowhere. A locked door has to say who opens it.
 *
 * Two states, because "Revoke All Access" now turns off the classroom flag too.
 * One locked feature on an otherwise open account sends the student back to
 * their classroom. A revoked account has no classroom to go back to, so
 * offering the button would loop them straight onto this same screen.
 */
export default function LockedModule() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();
  const canReachClassroom = permissions.has_classroom_access || permissions.is_admin;
  const canMessage = permissions.has_chat_access || permissions.is_admin;
''')
s=s.replace('''        <h1 className="mb-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">
          Not open yet
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          This part of the classroom isn't in your plan right now. If that looks
          wrong, send Joshua a message and he'll sort it out.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <Button onClick={() => navigate("/classroom")}>
            Back to your classroom
          </Button>
          {/* Only offered when the student can actually reach Messages.
              Otherwise the one next step we hand them is another locked door. */}
          {permissions.has_chat_access || permissions.is_admin ? (
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate("/inbox")}>
              Message Joshua
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>''','''        <h1 className="mb-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">
          {canReachClassroom ? "Not open yet" : "Your access is paused"}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          {canReachClassroom
            ? "This part of the classroom isn't in your plan right now. If that looks wrong, send Joshua a message and he'll sort it out."
            : "Your account is still here and so is your work. Joshua has paused access for now. Reach out to him and he'll turn it back on."}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
          {canReachClassroom ? (
            <Button onClick={() => navigate("/classroom")}>
              Back to your classroom
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          {/* Only offered when the student can actually reach Messages.
              Otherwise the one next step we hand them is another locked door. */}
          {canMessage ? (
            <Button variant={canReachClassroom ? "outline" : "default"} onClick={() => navigate("/inbox")}>
              Message Joshua
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>''')
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)
EOF

Make "Revoke All Access" actually revoke all access

Revoking left several doors open. Closed each one:

- The button kept has_classroom_access on, so assigned work, notes and
  recaps, item pages and uploads stayed reachable. It now turns every
  flag off. The /classroom route still admits any signed-in student so
  they land somewhere truthful, but the dashboard itself is gated.
- /guided/:assignmentId and /guided-drills/:slug had no guard at all,
  so every guided-drill link a student had ever been sent still ran.
- /conditional-indicators-refresher and /classroom/uploads passed any
  signed-in user.
- has_activity_access was missing from guard_profile_admin_fields(), so
  a student could turn it back on against their own profile row.
- Permissions were cached for five minutes with no invalidation, so a
  revoke took up to five minutes to bite. The hook now watches the
  student's own profiles row over realtime and refetches on focus.

LockedModule gains a revoked state: "Back to your classroom" would have
looped a revoked student onto the same screen, so it is not offered.

Verified: typecheck clean, build clean, full suite shows the same 11
pre-existing failures as origin/main and no new ones.

description: Open the pull request
command: gh pr create --base main --head fix/revoke-all-access --title 'Make "Revoke All Access" actually revoke all access' --body "$(cat <<'EOF'
`Revoke All Access` on `/teacher/students` did not revoke all access. This closes every gap found.

## What was still open after a revoke

| Gap | Effect |
|---|---|
| `has_classroom_access` was force-kept `true` | Assigned work, notes and recaps, item pages, TA assignments and uploads all stayed reachable |
| `/guided/:assignmentId` and `/guided-drills/:slug` had no guard at all | Every guided-drill link a student had ever been sent still ran |
| `/conditional-indicators-refresher` was `allowSignedIn` | Teaching content open to a revoked account |
| `/classroom/uploads` had no guard | Handed-in work and returned files stayed open |
| `has_activity_access` missing from `guard_profile_admin_fields()` | A student could flip it back on against their own profile row, since RLS lets them update it |
| Permissions cached 5 minutes, never invalidated | A revoke took up to 5 minutes to bite on an open tab |

## Changes

- `setAllAccess` turns every flag off, classroom included. `/classroom` still admits any signed-in student so they land somewhere truthful, but `Classroom` renders the locked notice instead of the dashboard.
- Route guards added for guided drills (drill, classroom or bootcamp is enough), the refresher, and uploads.
- Migration `20260922120000`: adds `has_activity_access` to the admin-fields trigger and puts `profiles` in the `supabase_realtime` publication.
- `useUserPermissions` subscribes to the student's own `profiles` row over realtime and invalidates on UPDATE, with a 60s stale window and focus refetch as fallback. One shared channel per user, not one per mounted route.
- `LockedModule` gets a revoked state. "Back to your classroom" would have looped a revoked student onto the same screen, so it is not offered; the copy becomes "Your access is paused".

## Verification

- `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean.
- Full suite: 4720 passed, same 11 files / 13 tests failing as `origin/main` (switchyard, activity-centre, booking, AssistantComposer, singleSessionCore). Baseline confirmed by running the suite on `origin/main` in the same worktree. No new failures.
- New tests: revoked Classroom shows the locked notice, admin still sees the home, revoked student gets the paused copy with no classroom loop, and the revoke payload asserts `has_classroom_access: false`.

## Note before merge

The migration has to reach the live database for the realtime invalidation to work. Backend is Lovable Cloud-managed, so that deploy is yours to trigger. Without it the fallback still applies: a revoke lands within 60 seconds or on the next tab focus.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
EOF
)" 2>&1 | tail -3

## assistant

You were right that it didn't actually revoke everything. It left six doors open. All are closed now.

**PR:** [vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db#129](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/129)

## What a "revoked" student could still do

| Gap | What stayed reachable |
|---|---|
| `has_classroom_access` was deliberately kept on | Assigned work, notes and recaps, item pages, TA assignments, uploads |
| `/guided/:assignmentId` and `/guided-drills/:slug` had **no guard at all** | Every guided-drill link they'd ever been sent still ran |
| `/conditional-indicators-refresher` admitted any signed-in user | Teaching content |
| `/classroom/uploads` had no guard | Handed-in and returned work |
| `has_activity_access` was missing from the DB trigger | A student could turn Activities back on themselves, since RLS lets them update their own profile row |
| Flags cached 5 minutes, never invalidated | A revoke took up to 5 minutes to bite on an open tab |

The first one was the big one. The old comment said it was intentional so the student "still has somewhere to stand" — but the classroom home *is* the coursework, so that reasoning kept the main room open.

## What it does now

Revoke turns off all 13 flags. The `/classroom` route still lets them land so they don't hit a redirect loop, but the page itself shows "Your access is paused" instead of their dashboard. Their login and all their work survive untouched.

I also fixed a dead end I created: "Back to your classroom" on the locked screen would have looped a revoked student straight back onto the same screen, so that button is hidden for them. If messages are off too, the screen just tells them to reach out to you.

Grant Full Access is unchanged and turns everything back on.

## Verified

Typecheck clean, build clean. Full suite: 4720 passing. There are 13 pre-existing failures (switchyard, activity-centre, booking, AssistantComposer) — I confirmed those by running the suite on a clean `origin/main` in the same worktree first, and they fail identically there. None are mine.

**What I did not verify:** the student-side locked screen in a real browser. That needs signing in as a revoked student, and I won't enter someone's password. The behavior is covered by tests instead — revoked student sees the locked notice, admin still sees the home, the revoke payload sends `has_classroom_access: false`.

## One thing needs you

The migration (`has_activity_access` trigger fix + putting `profiles` in the realtime publication) has to reach the live database. Backend is Lovable Cloud-managed, so per your rules that deploy is your call, not mine. Want me to send it to the Lovable agent?

Without it, the code still works — a revoke lands within 60 seconds or on the next tab focus rather than instantly, and the `has_activity_access` self-grant hole stays open until the trigger updates.

[/external_agent_tool_call]

[external_agent_tool_call: Bash]
description: Write the archive and delete migration
command: cat > supabase/migrations/20260922140000_student_archive_and_delete.sql <<'EOF'
-- Two ways to remove a student from the People page.
--
-- Archive is the everyday one: access off, hidden from the list, every record
-- kept, reversible. It is a profiles column, not a deletion.
--
-- Hard delete removes the auth user, which cascades to roughly sixty tables.
-- Two things must outlive it: Joshua's own session records, and the payment
-- and booking history. The booking tables already snapshot email and name, so
-- they read fine once detached. student_sessions did not: student_id was NOT
-- NULL and cascaded, so deleting a student destroyed the teaching record of
-- sessions he taught. That is his work product, not theirs.

-- ── Archive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ALTER TABLE public.profiles
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS archived_at timestamptz,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS archived_reason text;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.archived_at IS
  'Set when a student is archived: access revoked, hidden from the People list, records kept. NULL means active.';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS profiles_archived_at_idx
  ON public.profiles (archived_at) WHERE archived_at IS NOT NULL;

-- Archiving is an admin act, like the access flags, so students cannot clear
-- it on their own row through the "Students can update own profile" policy.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR
     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR
     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR
     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR
     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR
     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR
     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR
     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR
     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR
     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR
     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR
     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR
     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR
     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR
     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR
     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR
     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'profiles: access flags, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled';
  END IF;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$function$;

-- ── Who was deleted ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- The surviving rows keep the old uuid in former_student_id, which is a plain
-- column and not a foreign key. This table is what makes that uuid readable
-- again, so a detached session or payment still has a name on it.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.deleted_students (
  user_id      uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  email        text,
  display_name text,
  deleted_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_by   uuid,
  note         text
);

ALTER TABLE public.deleted_students ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Admins can view deleted students" ON public.deleted_students;
CREATE POLICY "Admins can view deleted students"
  ON public.deleted_students FOR SELECT
  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

-- ── Keep the session record ──────────────────────────────────────────────
ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;

ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions ALTER COLUMN student_id DROP NOT NULL;

DO $$
DECLARE fk_name text;
BEGIN
  SELECT c.conname INTO fk_name
  FROM pg_constraint c
  JOIN unnest(c.conkey) k(attnum) ON true
  JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.conrelid AND a.attnum = k.attnum
  WHERE c.contype = 'f'
    AND c.conrelid = 'public.student_sessions'::regclass
    AND c.confrelid = 'auth.users'::regclass
    AND a.attname = 'student_id'
  LIMIT 1;

  IF fk_name IS NOT NULL THEN
    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions DROP CONSTRAINT %I', fk_name);
  END IF;

  ALTER TABLE public.student_sessions
    ADD CONSTRAINT student_sessions_student_id_fkey
    FOREIGN KEY (student_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
END $$;

-- The booking and payment tables are already ON DELETE SET NULL and already
-- carry their own email/name snapshots. They only need the old id kept so the
-- rows can still be grouped per person after the account is gone.
ALTER TABLE public.booking_passes          ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.booking_commerce_events ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;
ALTER TABLE public.external_booking_events ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS former_student_id uuid;

-- ── Snapshot on the way out ──────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Runs BEFORE the delete, so it can still read the profile and still see the
-- rows that are about to be detached by the SET NULL cascades.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.snapshot_deleted_student()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.deleted_students (user_id, email, display_name, deleted_at)
  VALUES (
    OLD.id,
    OLD.email,
    (SELECT display_name FROM public.profiles WHERE class_id = OLD.id::text),
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
    SET email = EXCLUDED.email,
        display_name = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.display_name, public.deleted_students.display_name),
        deleted_at = EXCLUDED.deleted_at;

  UPDATE public.student_sessions          SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.booking_passes            SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.booking_commerce_events   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE user_id    = OLD.id;
  UPDATE public.external_booking_events   SET former_student_id = OLD.id WHERE student_id = OLD.id;

  RETURN OLD;
END;
$function$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS snapshot_deleted_student_trg ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER snapshot_deleted_student_trg
  BEFORE DELETE ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.snapshot_deleted_student();
EOF

description: Add archive, unarchive, and hard delete actions
command: python - <<'PYEOF'
p='supabase/functions/admin-manage-users/index.ts'
s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()

# 1. list: carry archive state through
s=s.replace('''        .select("class_id, display_name, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));
      if (pErr) throw pErr;''','''        .select("class_id, display_name, last_seen_at, archived_at, archived_reason, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));
      if (pErr) throw pErr;''')

# 2. new actions, inserted before the unknown-action fallthrough
old='''      return jsonRes({ error: "Unknown action" }, 400);'''
new='''      // Archive: the everyday way to remove someone from the People page.
      // Every door shut, stamped archived, nothing destroyed. Reversible.
      if (action === "archive_student") {
        const { class_id, reason = null } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const { data: target } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(class_id);
        if ((target?.user?.email || "").toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "The primary admin account cannot be archived." }, 403);
        }

        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {
          archived_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          archived_reason: reason,
        };
        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;

        const { data: archived, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update(updates)
          .eq("class_id", class_id)
          .select("class_id, display_name, archived_at");
        if (error) throw error;
        if (!archived || archived.length === 0) {
          console.error("archive_student: no profile matched", { class_id });
          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);
        }

        // Revoking flags is not enough on its own: an open tab holds a live
        // session. Ending it makes the archive take effect now rather than
        // whenever their token happens to expire.
        const { error: signOutError } = await admin.auth.admin.signOut(class_id, "global");
        if (signOutError) {
          console.warn("archive_student: sign-out failed", { class_id, error: signOutError.message });
        }

        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: archived[0] });
      }

      // Unarchive puts them back in the list. Access stays off on purpose:
      // coming back is a decision about which doors to reopen, and silently
      // restoring all thirteen would be a bigger act than the button implies.
      if (action === "unarchive_student") {
        const { class_id } = body;
        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);

        const { data: restored, error } = await admin
          .from("profiles")
          .update({ archived_at: null, archived_reason: null })
          .eq("class_id", class_id)
          .select("class_id, display_name, archived_at");
        if (error) throw error;
        if (!restored || restored.length === 0) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);
        }
        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: restored[0] });
      }

      // Hard delete. Removes the auth user, which cascades to roughly sixty
      // tables. Session records and booking/payment rows are detached rather
      // than destroyed by the snapshot_deleted_student trigger.
      //
      // Guarded three ways, because this cannot be undone: the caller must
      // name the account by email exactly, the primary admin is untouchable,
      // and an admin cannot delete themselves.
      if (action === "delete_student") {
        const { class_id, confirm_email } = body;
        if (!class_id || typeof confirm_email !== "string") {
          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);
        }

        const { data: target, error: lookupError } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(class_id);
        if (lookupError || !target?.user) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "No account matched that id" }, 404);
        }
        const email = (target.user.email || "").toLowerCase();

        if (email === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "The primary admin account cannot be deleted." }, 403);
        }
        if (class_id === callerId) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "You cannot delete your own account." }, 403);
        }
        if (email !== confirm_email.trim().toLowerCase()) {
          return jsonRes({ error: "The typed email does not match that account." }, 400);
        }

        // Stamp who did it before the row goes; the trigger fills in the rest.
        const { error: deleteError } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(class_id);
        if (deleteError) {
          console.error("delete_student: delete failed", { class_id, error: deleteError });
          throw deleteError;
        }

        const { error: stampError } = await admin
          .from("deleted_students")
          .update({ deleted_by: callerId })
          .eq("user_id", class_id);
        if (stampError) {
          console.warn("delete_student: could not stamp deleted_by", { class_id, error: stampError.message });
        }

        return jsonRes({ success: true, deleted_email: email });
      }

      return jsonRes({ error: "Unknown action" }, 400);'''

  // ends their session, keeps every record, and can be undone.
  const setArchived = async (person: PeopleRow, archived: boolean) => {
    const who = person.display_name || person.email;
    if (archived && !window.confirm(
      `Archive ${who}?\\n\\n` +
      'Their access is turned off and they come off your list. Nothing is ' +
      'deleted: their work, sessions and history all stay, and you can bring ' +

          class_id: person.class_id,

      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      toast.success(archived ? `${who} is archived` : `${who} is back on your list`);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('setArchived failed', { class_id: person.class_id, archived, error });

  // email to be typed rather than accepting a single click. Session records and
  // payment history are detached and survive; everything else goes.

    const typed = window.prompt(
      `Permanently delete ${who}?\\n\\n` +

      'flagged questions and assigned work are all destroyed. Your session ' +
      'records and their payment history are kept and detached.\\n\\n' +
      `To confirm, type their email exactly:\\n${person.email}`,
    );
    if (typed === null) return;
    if (typed.trim().toLowerCase() !== person.email.toLowerCase()) {
      toast.error('That email did not match. Nothing was deleted.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { action: 'delete_student', class_id: person.class_id, confirm_email: typed.trim() },

      if (res.error) throw res.error;
      toast.success(`${who} has been deleted`);
      await fetchData();

      toast.error('Failed to delete the account');

  };

import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DeskPeopleSurface } from './DeskPeopleSurface';

const { invokeMock, getSessionMock, toastErrorMock } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  invokeMock: vi.fn(),
  getSessionMock: vi.fn(),
  toastErrorMock: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock('@/integrations/supabase/client', () => ({
  supabase: {
    auth: {
      getSession: getSessionMock,
    },
    functions: {
      invoke: invokeMock,
    },
  },
}));

vi.mock('@/hooks/useHubStudents', () => ({
  useHubStudents: () => ({
    rows: [
      {
        class_id: 'student-1',
        display_name: 'Meghan Mullins',
        email: 'meghan@example.com',
        last_seen_at: '2026-06-18T12:58:00.000Z',
        role: 'user',
        has_bootcamp_access: true,
        has_classroom_access: true,
        has_analytics_access: true,
        has_schedule_access: true,
        has_practice_access: true,
        has_drill_access: true,
        has_waj_access: true,
        has_flagged_access: true,
        has_chat_access: true,
        has_export_access: false,
        has_ta_access: false,
        assignments_total: 4,
        assignments_assigned: 0,
        assignments_viewed: 1,
        assignments_completed: 3,
        has_unread_from_student: true,
      },
    ],
    loading: false,
    error: null,
    refresh: vi.fn(),
  }),
}));

vi.mock('sonner', () => ({
  toast: {
    error: toastErrorMock,
    success: vi.fn(),
=====TAIL=====
    // The simplified People page only manages Drill access plus role. No
    // broad permission matrix, templates, or bulk grant/revoke.
    expect(screen.queryByText('Templates')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Role Management')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Protected primary admin')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows one whole-account access control per student', async () => {
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');

    expect(within(row).getByText(/Drill on/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Revoke All Access/i })).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Bootcamps' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Classroom' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('switch', { name: 'Practice' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('revokes every feature except the classroom home page', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Revoke All Access/i }));

    const call = invokeMock.mock.calls.find(([, options]) => options?.body?.action === 'apply_template');
    expect(call).toBeTruthy();
    const flags = call![1].body.flags as Record<string, boolean>;
    expect(call![1].body.class_id).toBe('student-1');
    expect(flags.has_classroom_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_drill_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_chat_access).toBe(false);
    expect(flags.has_practice_access).toBe(false);
  });

  it('does nothing when the confirmation is dismissed', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(false);
    renderPeople();

    const row = await screen.findByTestId('people-row-student-1');
    fireEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: /Revoke All Access/i }));

    expect(invokeMock.mock.calls.some(([, options]) => options?.body?.action === 'apply_template')).toBe(false);
  });
});

function renderPeople() {
  const queryClient = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false }, mutations: { retry: false } } });
  // Roster names are links into the student dossier (T3.2), so the surface needs
  // a router in scope.
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <DeskPeopleSurface />
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

const users = [
  {
    class_id: 'student-1',
    email: 'meghan@example.com',
    display_name: 'Meghan Mullins',
    role: 'user',
    last_seen_at: '2026-06-18T12:58:00.000Z',
    has_bootcamp_access: true,
    has_classroom_access: true,
    has_analytics_access: true,
    has_schedule_access: true,
    has_practice_access: true,
    has_drill_access: true,
    has_waj_access: true,
    has_flagged_access: true,
    has_chat_access: true,
    has_export_access: false,
    has_ta_access: false,
  },
  {
    class_id: 'admin-1',
    email: 'contact@aspiringattorneys.com',
    display_name: 'Joshua',
    role: 'admin',
    last_seen_at: '2026-06-18T12:00:00.000Z',
    has_bootcamp_access: true,
    has_classroom_access: true,
    has_analytics_access: true,
    has_schedule_access: true,
    has_practice_access: true,
    has_drill_access: true,
    has_waj_access: true,
    has_flagged_access: true,
    has_chat_access: true,
    has_export_access: true,
    has_ta_access: true,
  },
];

describe('DeskPeopleSurface', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-06-18T13:00:00.000Z'));
    invokeMock.mockClear();
    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ data: { session: { user: { id: 'admin-1' } } } });
    invokeMock.mockImplementation((_name, options) => {
      if (options?.headers?.['x-mode'] === 'analytics') {
        return Promise.resolve({
          data: {
            total_users: 2,
            active_today: 2,
            active_week: 2,
            admin_count: 1,
            feature_adoption: {},
            total_attempts: 0,
            week_attempts: 0,
          },
          error: null,
        });
      }
      if (options?.method === 'POST') {
        const { class_id, field, value } = options.body as Record<string, unknown>;
        return Promise.resolve({
          data: { success: true, profile: { class_id, [field as string]: value } },
          error: null,
        });
      }
      return Promise.resolve({ data: users, error: null });
    });
  });

Two ways to take a student off the People page, deliberately different in weight.

## Archive (the everyday one)

Turns off all 13 access flags, stamps `archived_at`, and removes them from the list and the header count. Nothing is destroyed and it is reversible. It lands on an open tab immediately, because `profiles` is now in the realtime publication.

Unarchive puts them back with access still off, so reopening doors stays a deliberate choice rather than a side effect.

An `Archived` filter chip shows them when you want them.

## Delete permanently (the exception)

Removes the auth user, which cascades to roughly sixty tables. Two things outlive it:

- **Your session records.** `student_sessions.student_id` was `NOT NULL` and cascaded, so deleting a student destroyed the teaching record of sessions you taught. It is now nullable, `ON DELETE SET NULL`, and carries `former_student_id`.
- **Payment and booking history.** `booking_passes`, `booking_commerce_events`, `single_session_bookings` and `external_booking_events` were already `SET NULL` and already snapshot email and name. They now keep `former_student_id` so rows can still be grouped per person.

A `BEFORE DELETE` trigger on `auth.users` writes identity into the new `deleted_students` table and backfills `former_student_id` before the cascades fire, so a detached session or payment still has a name against it.

## Guards

- The email must be typed and match exactly, or nothing happens.
- The primary admin cannot be deleted or archived.
- An admin cannot delete themselves.
- `archived_at` and `archived_reason` are admin-controlled in `guard_profile_admin_fields()`, so a student cannot unarchive themselves through the self-update RLS policy.

## Note

I dropped a planned `auth.admin.signOut(class_id)` call during review: that API takes a JWT, not a user id, so it would have silently failed on every archive. The realtime invalidation is what actually makes an archive immediate, and the UI copy says only what is true.

- `tsc --noEmit` clean, `deno check` clean on the edge function, `npm run build` clean.
- Full suite: 4724 passed, same pre-existing failures as `main` (switchyard, activity-centre, booking, AssistantComposer), no new ones.
- New tests: archived students stay off the list until the filter asks for them, archiving calls `archive_student`, and delete refuses a mismatched email then goes through on an exact match.

## Before this works live

The migration has to be applied to the database, and the frontend has to be published. Both are yours to trigger.

PRBODY

Both lookups are running. Here's the frontier that doesn't depend on them.

❓ **Q1** — **What a tier actually is**: Today the app enforces access through 13 boolean columns on `profiles`, and everything reads them — `ProtectedRoute`, the classroom rail, the Home dashboard. A tier could either (a) be a label that *computes* those 13 flags, leaving the flags as the enforcement layer, or (b) *replace* the flags with a single `access_tier` column that every gate checks instead.

Option (b) is cleaner on paper but means touching every gate in the app, and it can't express Tier 2 at all, since Tier 2 is by definition "these specific features and not those."

➡️ **(a) Tier computes the flags.** Add one `access_tier` column that records *why* a student's flags look the way they do. Tier 1 sets all 13 on, Tier 2 is whatever you toggle, Tier 3 sets them off and leans on assignment. Nothing downstream changes, the instant-toggle behaviour you want already works, and Tier 2 needs no new machinery.

---

❓ **Q2** — **One tier at a time, or stacking?**: Is a student in exactly one tier, or can tiers layer (e.g. Tier 3 assigned-only *plus* two features from Tier 2)?

➡️ **Exactly one tier at a time.** Stacking gets confusing fast and you already have a free-form way to express any combination: Tier 2 *is* the arbitrary case. If a Tier 3 student needs drill switched on, that's just moving them to Tier 2 with drill on.

---

❓ **Q3** — **Where does a brand-new signup land?**: Right now every access column defaults to `true`, so a stranger who signs up gets the entire platform. That is the same open-door problem we found this morning, one layer up. When tiers exist, what tier does someone land in the moment they create an account, before you've done anything?

➡️ **Tier 3 (assigned-only).** Nothing is open until you open it, which makes every grant deliberate. They can still sign in, finish the intake, message you and book. The alternative is a "no tier / prospect" state, but that's really just Tier 3 by another name.

---

❓ **Q4** — **When does Tier 1's full access end?**: You said one booked session buys full access, then started to say "10 days" and pulled back. So: does Tier 1 access ever expire on its own? Options: never expires and you downgrade by hand; expires a fixed number of days after the session; or lasts while they have unused session credit and drops when credits hit zero.

➡️ **Never auto-expires; you downgrade by hand.** Automatic revocation is the dangerous one: it fires silently, and the failure mode is a paying student losing their work mid-drill with no warning and no explanation. A stale grant costs you nothing by comparison. If you do want expiry later, it should warn you first and never act on its own.

---

❓ **Q5** — **What a Tier 3 student sees with nothing assigned**: If someone is assigned-only and you haven't assigned anything yet, what's on their screen? Everything locked including the classroom home, or a floor that always stays open?

➡️ **A floor of three: classroom home, messages, schedule.** They need somewhere to land, a way to reach you, and a way to book. That's exactly what `reset_onboarding` already keeps open, so it's an established precedent in the product rather than a new rule. Everything else stays shut until assigned.

# Context Map

The repo has more than one bounded context, each with its own language. This map points to where each glossary lives.

## Contexts

- [Public surface](./src/pages/CONTEXT.md) — the unauthenticated landing + sign-in page (`Auth.tsx`): how LSAT U describes itself to a prospective student.
- [Teacher workspace](./src/pages/teacher/CONTEXT.md) — Joshua's admin operator surface (`/teacher`): assigning prepared material to students and the transcript pipeline.
- [Teacher Desk page](./src/pages/teacher/desk/CONTEXT.md) — the named visual regions of the desk page itself (the boxes on screen), nested under the teacher workspace. Names the layout so we can revise it precisely.
- [Guided Drills & Explanations](./src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md) — the practice domain: guided drills, the question bank, drill instruction cards, and per-question explanations (the Phase 2 authoring work).
- [Classroom](./src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md) — the student's own room: the Classroom frame, its sections, and the flag-versus-wrong-answer distinction.
- [Reading Comprehension](./src/features/rc/CONTEXT.md) — full-passage RC drilling: passages, sessions, attempts, reveal modes, status, and the adaptive picker.
- [Activity Centre](./src/features/activity-centre/CONTEXT.md) — coach-led skill exercises a student is assigned, and the Read, Recall, Colour drill that runs inside one: Chunk, Detail, Coverage, The Recall, Report Card.
- [Booking](./src/components/booking/CONTEXT.md) — paying for and scheduling tutoring time: Packages, Booking Passes, the public microsite, and the Single Session (Slots, Holds, Bookers).

## Relationships

- Both surfaces describe the same product but to different audiences (prospective student vs. operator), so a word can carry different weight in each. Check the right glossary before reusing a term.
- A **flag** (Classroom) and a **wrong answer** (Wrong Answer Journal) are different things on different tables: a flag is chosen mid-question, a wrong answer is earned by missing one. Never collapse the two, and never let "flagged" drift into meaning "struggled with".
- **Set** now means one thing everywhere: the group a student assembles and drills. It used to mean a passage's position within its section in Reading Comprehension; that sense is now called **Position** (the data still carries `setNumber`). The collision was retired when the LR and RC pickers merged into one surface — a primary noun that changed meaning when you flipped the mode was the confusion the merge existed to remove. See `docs/adr/0006-rc-selection-is-question-grain.md`.
[/external_agent_tool_result]

# Teacher Workspace — Context

The teacher workspace (`/teacher`, admin-only) is Joshua's operator surface for running 1:1 LSAT bootcamps. This file is the glossary: what each term means here, so the nav and code stop colliding. It is not a spec.

## Organizing principle

The nav is **verb-first**: organized by what Joshua does, not by artifact type. The central verb is **Assign** — pushing prepared material to a student. The long-term goal is to make assignment fast to fire and eventually automated (driven by session transcripts), pulling from a library of prepared materials.

## Glossary

### Assignable (material type)
A kind of prepared material Joshua can give a student. These stay SEPARATE object types — they are NOT collapsed into one generic item. The types: **Bootcamp**, **Guided Drill**, **Question Set**, **Note**, **Homework**, and (future) **Whiteboard Template**. Each keeps its own builder/editor. What unifies them is not their shape but two shared surfaces: the **Library** (where all of them live) and **Assign** (how any of them reach a student).

### Library (content store)
The single place that holds ALL of Joshua's prepared materials, grouped by type: every Bootcamp, every Guided Drill, every Question Set, every Note, every Whiteboard Template. This is a CONTENT store, not an action menu. (The current app misuses "Library" as a wrapper for assign-actions; that is the bug being fixed.) From the Library Joshua browses/edits material and can assign it.

### Assign (the verb / action)
The action of pushing a Library item to one student with a date. In the redesigned Teacher Workspace, Assign is a top-level area because assigning work is one of Joshua's primary daily jobs. It still has **two doors**, co-equal: (1) from the **Library** — "I have this drill, send it to Lydia"; (2) from a **Student** — "here's Lydia, add a conditional note + the Causation bootcamp." Both doors land in the student's plan/feed. Future: pre-filled / automated from session transcripts.

## Top-level nav (target)

Verb/surface-first, co-equal Library + Students:
- **Today** — the start page for what needs attention now.
- **Students** — door 1: roster -> per-student plan, assign from here, recap, smartboard, conversation, that student's Insights.
- **Assign** — the fast path for giving work to students.
- **Teach** — live teaching tools, teaching materials, review surfaces, and the AI Teaching Assistant.
- **Admin** — accounts + granular feature flags.

Homes still TBD under these areas for: Schedule, Whiteboard, Library, Insights, Conversations, Recaps, Templates, Bootcamp References, Tutor Corpus.

### Bootcamp
A multi-day topic program (Causation Station, Intro to LR, etc.). It is ONE kind of Assignable. NOTE the old collision: two surfaces were both called "Bootcamps" —
- the **assign** action (`TeacherBootcamps`, toggle a bootcamp on for a student) → this is just Assign.
- the **analytics** view (`BootcampsHub`, per-topic completion/accuracy) → this is **Insights**, NOT Assign.
"Bootcamp" the word now means only the program itself. The two jobs are named by their verb.

### Insights
Read-only analytics: per-student and per-topic progress, completion, drill accuracy, flashcard reviews. Absorbs the old top-level "Bootcamps" (topic analytics) and the "Analytics" Library tab.

### Bootcamp Reference
Inspiration material **for Joshua only** (`data/bootcampReferences.ts`): example bootcamps and sources he draws from when *building* his own bootcamps. **Never assigned to students.** This is the hard line that defines the Library: References are teaching-prep for Joshua, so they do NOT belong in the Library (which holds only assignable, student-facing material). References get their own separate top-level home (a personal reference shelf). Only **Bootcamps** — the finished programs — are assignable.

### Library (rule)
**Library holds only things a student can receive.** If Joshua can't assign it to a student, it is not Library material (th...

## assistant

## Where we are

We design three access tiers for students. I asked you five questions. You did not answer them. This is the same set of questions in simple words.

## The background

LSAT U controls student access with 13 switches. Every student record has these switches. One switch opens the Drill. One switch opens the Classroom. Other switches open the other features.

All 13 switches are ON for a new student today. This is a problem. A new person gets the full platform at the first sign-in.

A **tier** is a name for one set of switch positions. You want three tiers:

- **Tier 1** — the student books one Single Session. The student then gets all features.
- **Tier 2** — you select each feature. You can change a feature at any time.
- **Tier 3** — the student opens only the material that you **Assign**.

Two searches of the code are not complete. They will tell us how the system knows about a new Booking Pass. They will also tell us how **Assigned work** gets to a student. You do not need this data to answer the five questions.

## The five questions

**Q1. Does a tier set the 13 switches, or does a tier replace them?**

All parts of the app read the 13 switches now. A replacement is a large change. A replacement also cannot show Tier 2, because Tier 2 is a free selection of features.

➡️ Keep the 13 switches. The tier sets them. Add one new field for the tier name.

**Q2. Can a student have more than one tier at the same time?**

➡️ No. Give one tier to one student. Tier 2 can already show any set of features.

**Q3. Which tier do you give to a new student at the first sign-in?**

➡️ Give Tier 3. Then nothing is open until you open it. The student can still sign in, complete the intake, send you a message, and book a session.

**Q4. Does Tier 1 access stop automatically after some days?**

➡️ No. You remove the access manually. An automatic stop is dangerous. It can close the platform for a student in the middle of a Drill. The student gets no warning.

**Q5. What can a Tier 3 student open when you assign no material?**

➡️ Three items stay open: the Classroom home page, the messages, and the schedule. The student needs a place to land, a way to contact you, and a way to book a session. Everything else stays closed.

Answer these five. Then I continue with the next set.
